[\[252b.1\]]chub sems dpa' la thog ma med pa'i 'khor ba na thams cad mkhyen par yongs su ma bsngos pa'i sbyin pa btang ba yang /_thabs mkhas pas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la byed cing bsngo na pha rol tu phyin pa'i grangs su 'gro'o/_/byang chub sems dpas thog ma kho nar sbyor ba'i pha rol tu phyin pa la bslab 
[\[252b.2\]]par bya'o/_/nyams pa phyir bslang bar nus sam/_smras pa/_ji ste yang dag pa'i mtha' mngon sum du ma byas la/_ji ste bden pa ma mthong na'o/_/gal te byang chub sems dpa' gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi bskal par khyim par gnas te 'dod pa'i yon tan rnams 
[\[252b.3\]]la brten cing longs spyod la/_nyan thos sam rang sangs rgyas su sems mi bskyed na/_de tsam gyis ni byang chub sems dpa' tshul khrims 'chal pa zhes mi bya ste/_'di ltar lhag pa'i sems ma dkrugs pa'i phyir ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' 'gog pa'i snyoms par 'jug 
[\[252b.4\]]pa la dbang thob mod kyi/_mnyam par gzhog par ni mi byed do/_/'du shes dang tshor ba bar gzhag na ni/_dgra bcom pa'i 'bras bu mngon sum du byed par blta ste/_yab dang sras mjal ba las/_bcom ldan 'das kyis gsung gcig brjod pa las/_skye bo bsam pa ji bzhin so sor 
[\[252b.5\]]'chal/_bskal pa grangs med pa gzhal du med pa'i sngon rol na/_de bzhin gshegs pa dbang po'i tog ces bya ba 'jig rten du byung ste/_chos kun yid la byar med pa/_/'di yang gzungs kyi sgo yin te/_/bde bar gshegs pas gzungs su bstan/_/stobs bcu rgyal ba ma 
[\[252b.6\]]dris par/_/sras mchog byang chub lung mi ston/_/de bzhin gshegs pas kun rdzob dang don dam pa gnyis thugs su chud de zhes bya ba yang 'dir zad do/_/de yang bcom ldan 'das kyis stong pa nyid rab tu gzigs/_rab tu mkhyen/_legs par mngon du mdzad pas/_de'i 
[\[252b.7\]]phyir thams cad mkhyen pa zhes bya'o/_/'dod chags gnyen por zhi gnas bka' stsal pa/_zhe sdang gnyen por byams pa gsungs/_/gti mug gnyen por lhag mthong ni/_/thub pa mchog gis sems can rnams la bstan/_/de bzhin gshegs pa ni ma skyes te/_chos thams cad 
[\[253a\]]
[\[253a.1\]]kyang ma skyes pa'o/_/de bas na chos thams cad ni de bzhin gshegs pa'o/_/dmyal ba nas tshe 'phos pa'i rtags skad che ba yin/_skad lhang lhang po yin/_sems can kun la yid mi rton pa yin no/_/dud 'gro'i skye gnas nas shi 'phos pa'i rtags ni gnyid 
[\[253a.2\]]che le lo che mang du za ba yin/_mi gsal bar smra ba yin/_kun du bskums te nyal ba yin/_gzhan du smra zhing gzhan du skye zhing g.yo byed pa yin/_bab chol du spyod pa yin no/_/gshin rje'i 'jig rten nas shi 'phos pa'i rtags ni skra ser rtag tu bkres skom ser sna dang 'dod zhen che ba/_dngos 
[\[253a.3\]]po kun la rku ba'i sems yod la/_gzhan gyi rnyed pa la phrag dog che ba yin no/_/lha ma yin las shi 'phos pa ni/_khengs pa/_'thab krol can/_khon du 'dzin pa/_nga rgyal can/_lus che ba/_mche ba snang ba/_dbyen la dga' ba/_kun la brnyas par byed pa yin no/_/mi las shi 'phos pa ni/_
[\[253a.4\]]'phags pa la brten pa/_'phags pa la smon pa/_khrel dang ngo tsha shes pa/_mdza' bo la gdung ba/_gtong phod pa/_skad thams cad 'dzin pa'i mthu yod pa yin no/_/lha las tshe 'phos pa ni mdzes pa gtsang sbra che ba/_rol mo'i sgra la dga' ba/_byams pa che ba'i ngang tshul can/_'jam 
[\[253a.5\]]par smra ba/_smra mkhas pa/_phan par bya ba la mkhas pa yin no/_/gang rnam par shes pa tha ma 'gag pa de ni 'chi ba zhes bya/_gang rnam par shes pa dang po 'byung ba de ni skye ba zhes bya'o/_/rgyal po nga las nu byung ba'i tshe 'dzam gling gi grong khyer thams cad bya gag gi 'phur stabs kyis chod pa 
[\[253a.6\]]tsam du nye'o/_/bsod nams thams cad sdud pa'i ting nge 'dzin las/_bsod nams su bya ba thams cad ni/_byang chub tu sems bskyed pa de'i khongs su 'dus shing rjes su zhugs pa'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis/_byang chub sems dpa' gang la 'dug pa'i 'jig rten gyi 
[\[253a.7\]]khams de rdo rje'i rang bzhin du byin gyis ma brlabs na/_skyes ma thag tu gom pa bdun 'dor ba'i tshe/_thur du dpag tshad drug khri brgyad stong zhom par 'gyur te/_/'og nas chu shing rta'i gnya' shing tsam gyi rgyun dag 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i yab yum las skyes 
[\[253b\]]
[\[253b.1\]]pa'i stobs kyang /_mi bcu'i stobs nas/_ba glang dang /_lha dang /_byang chub sems dpa' la sogs pa bcu bcur bsgres pa/_drug cu la thug pa bas kyang che ste/_tha ma ni byang chub sems dpa' skye ba gcig gis thogs pa yin par 'byung /_byang chub sems dpa'i tshul khrims

[\[253b.4\]]ni/_ri dang /_ri sul dang /_shing dag gi nang du chos kyi gter bcug pa yod de gzungs dang /_chos kyi sgo mtha' yas pa glegs bam du byas pa dag kyang lag tu 'ong bar 'gyur ro/_/chos bzhi dang ldan na byang chub kyi sems brjed par 'gyur te/_lhag pa'i nga rgyal

[\[254a\]]
[\[254a.1\]]la g.yos ston pa dang /_dge ba'i bshes gnyen la lce gnyis byed pa dang /_rnyed pa dang bkur sti la chags pa'o/_/spyod pa brgyad khri bzhi stong ni 'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug cha mnyam pa la spyod pa nyi khri chig stong ngo /_/gal te byang chub sems dpa' lta ba 'ga'

[\[254b.1\]]mug ma bsal cig_/rang gi lus las gyen du ma bskyod cig_/mi dge ba spyod cig_/lta ba tshar ma bcad cig_/kun du mi sbyor ba ma ston cig_/phung po rnams zung shig_/khams rnams ril por gyis shig_/skye mched rnams la spyod cig_/mi dge ba la rtogs shig_/dge ba spongs shig_/

[\[255a.1\]]phra mas phye nas yang dag pa'i lam la bkod pas phra ma'o/_/rgol ba tshar bcad pas tshig rtsub pa'o/_/bye ba 'dum par byed pas na kyal pa'o/_/sems can kun bde ba la bkod par 'dod pas brnab sems so/_/chos 'jig pa'i tshe

[\[255b.1\]]bsngal gyi grogs ma yin pa'o/_/phung po gsum pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs kha don bya'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' byams pa dang /_spyod pa rnam par dag pa la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/khyed gnyis kyis khyim bdag 'di rnams rab me tog puN+Da rI ka zhes 'gro

[\[256a\]]
[\[256a.1\]]zhing gi yon tan rlom sems gang med pa/_/skyes bu khyu mchog zhing du de 'gro'o/_/'phags pa 'od dpag med kyi bkod pa las/_de bzhin gshegs pa de spyi ni sku tshe bskal pa dpag tu med pas tshe dpag med ces kyang bya/_mngon par sangs rgyas

[\[256b\]]
[\[256b.1\]]pa las ma byin na sems can dmyal bar 'gro/_/byang chub sems dpa'i so sor thar pa las/_gang byin pa de ni sangs rgyas rnams kyis bsngags pa'o/_/gang bzhag pa de ni byis pa rnams kyis bsngags pa'o/_/sred med kyi bus zhus pa las/_dngos po gang la gtong ba'i blo mi skye ba dang /_sems mi

[\[257a\]]
[\[257a.1\]]su 'jug pa dang /_mtshams med pa gang yang rung ba zhig byed pa dang /_'jig rten pha rol la g.yem ste/_mi dge ba bcu yang blangs shing gzhan yang len du 'jug pa'o/_/sa'i snying po las/_ngan phyir rab tu byung ba tshul khrims dang ldan pa'am/_'chal ba 
[\[257a.2\]]yang rung ste/_'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po yang chos dang mthun pas kyang chad pas gcod du mi gnang na/_chos ma yin pas lta smos ci dgos/_lhag pa'i bsam pas bskul ba las/_byams pa gang dag la brtson pa med pa dang /_bsam gtan dang /_spong ba dang /_kha ton 
[\[257a.3\]]bya ba dang mang du thos pa tshol ba med pa de dag ni/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i bstan pa la rab tu byung ba ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i bstan pa ni/_bsam gtan la sogs pas rab tu phye ba yin gyi/_'di ltar zhal ta byed pa dang /_las kyi mthas rab tu 
[\[257a.4\]]phye ba ni ma yin no/_/byams pa zhal ta byed pa la brtson pa'i byang chub sems dpas rin po che sna bdun gyi mchod rten rnams kyis stong gsum gyi stong chen po bkang yang /_des nga mnyes pa dang /_rim gro dang /_bsti stang byas par mi 'gyur ro/_/dam pa'i chos 
[\[257a.5\]]dran pa nye bar gzhag pa las/_lkog tu lhag mar bya ba te lhag ma mi za bdag la byin na [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]su skye'o/_/'jam dpal rnam par rol pa'i mdo las/_khong khrob khong khro ba zhes bya ba ni/_bskal pa brgyar bsags pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba 'joms pas de'i phyir khong khro ba zhes 
[\[257a.6\]]bya'o/_/blo gros mi zad pa las/_dge ba'i rtsa ba byang chub tu yongs su bsngos ba ni byang chub kyi snying po la 'dug gi bar du bar ma dor zad par mi 'gyur ro/_/btsun pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu 'di lta ste dper na/_chu'i thigs pa rgya mtsho chen por ltung ba ni/_bskal pa'i mthar thug gi bar du 
[\[257a.7\]]bar ma dor zad pa med do/_/nye ba 'khor gyis zhus pa las/_nye ba 'khor gal te theg pa chen po la zhugs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' la/_'dod chags dang ldan pa'i ltung ba gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed byung ba dang /_gang zhe sdang dang ldan pa'i ltung ba gcig 'byung ba/_byang chub sems 
[\[257b\]]
[\[257b.1\]]dpa'i theg pa brtsan par bya na/_de gnyis las gang zhe sdang dang ldan pa ni shin tu lci'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_zhe sdang ni sems can yongs su gtong bar 'gyur/_'dod chags ni sems can sdud par 'gyur ba'i phyir ro/_/nye ba 'khor de la nyon mongs pa gang sems can sdud par 'gyur ba de la ni/_
[\[257b.2\]]byang chub sems dpa' mdzes pa yang med/_'jigs pa yang med do/_/nye ba 'khor de lta bas na khyod la 'dod chags dang ldan pa'i ltung ba thams cad ni ltung ba ma yin par ngas bshad do/_/nye ba 'khor de la gang thabs la mi mkhas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' de dag ni/_'dod chags 
[\[257b.3\]]dang ldan pas 'jigs kyi/_zhe sdang dang ldan pas ma yin no/_/thabs mkhas pa de dag ni zhe sdang dang ldan pas 'jigs kyi/_'dod chags dang ldan pas ma yin no/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mdzod kyi mdo las/_chos 'dod pa rnams rnam par g.yeng bar byed pa 'di ni/_tshig kyal pa'i 
[\[257b.4\]]nang na ma rungs pa'o/_/zla ba sgron ma'i mdo las/_chos kyi rnam grangs ji snyed mang por chub byas kyang /_/thos pas rgyags te tshul khrims srung bar mi byed na/_/'di ni thos pas yongs su bskyab par mi nus te/_/tshul khrims 'chal ba des na ngan song 'gro bar 'gyur/_/'phags pa blo gros 
[\[257b.5\]]mi zad pa'i mdo las/_de la sems can la dmigs pa'i byams pa ni gang thog mar snying du sdug pa'i sems can la phan pa dang /_bde ba nye bar sgrub pa'i bsam pa goms par byed cing /_de dang mthun pa'i byams pa nye bar sgrub po/_/de'i 'og tu 'dres pa rnams la'o/_/de'i 'og tu nye 
[\[257b.6\]]'khor ba rnams la'o/_/de'i 'og tu rang gi grong na gnas pa rnams la'o/_/de'i 'og tu gzhan gyi grong nas phyogs gcig gi bar la mos par byas shing khyab par byas nas rdzogs par byas te gnas so/_/phyogs bcur yang de bzhin no/_/dpal byin gyis zhus pa las/_
[\[257b.7\]]dper na byis pa sol ba thogs nas su/_/'di ni dkar por bya zhes bdar byed kyang /_/dkar po'i dngos por mi 'gyur zad par 'gyur/_/byis blo phyin ci log gi nga rgyal can/_/de ltar mi gang gtsang sbra'i blo gros ldan/_/nga yis lus 'di gtsang mar bya zhes te/_/bskus phyis 'dab 
[\[258a\]]
[\[258a.1\]]stegs brgyar ni khrus kyang byas/_/'chi bdag dbang gis zad 'gyur gtsang mi 'gyur/_/drag shul can gyis zhus pa las/_chang yang sbyin par 'byung /_mtshon cha la sogs pa yang shugs su 'gyur ba lta brtags te byin na ltung ba 'di nyid kyis 
[\[258a.2\]]mngon no/_/gtsug na rin po che las/_rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa'i stong pa nyid gang zhe na/_sbyin pa ma tshang ba med pa nas/_zhi gnas lhag mthong ma tshang ba med pa'i bar dang /_sems can yongs su smin par bya ba la rtag tu 'bad pa ste/_'di ni rnam 
[\[258a.3\]]pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa'i stong pa nyid do zhes bya ba nas/_rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems dpa'i bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i spyod pa yongs su dag pa'o zhes bya ba'i bar du 'byung ngo /_/'phags pa shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa las/_byang chub sems 
[\[258a.4\]]dpa' rnams ni snying rje med par ci yang mi spyod do/_/bslab pa kun las btus pa las byung ba/_de la phung po gsum ni sdig pa bshags pa dang /_bsod nams kyi rjes su yi rang ba dang /_rang sangs rgyas sa la bskul ba zhes bya ste/_bsod nams kyi phung po yin pa'i phyir ro/_/lang 
[\[258a.5\]]kar gshegs pa las/_blo gros chen po de la 'dod chen po rnams kyi thar par mi 'dod pa ji ltar 'byung zhe na/_'di lta ste/_dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad spangs pa dang /_thog ma med pa'i dus kyi sems can thams cad kyi phyir smin pa'o/_/de la dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad spangs pa gang zhe na/_
[\[258a.6\]]'di ltar byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod la 'di dag ni thar pa dang mthun pa'i mdo dang 'dul ba ma yin no zhes smra bas spong zhing skur ba yin te/_dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad spangs pas mya ngan las mi 'da'o/_/blo gros chen po gnyis su ni/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen 
[\[258a.7\]]po 'di ltar sems can thams cad yongs su mya ngan las ma 'das kyi bar du/_bdag kyang mya ngan las mi 'da'o zhes srid par smon lam dang thabs yongs su btang bas/_de'i phyir mya ngan las mi 'da'o/_/blo gros chen po de ni yongs su mya ngan las mi 'da' ba rnams kyi rang 
[\[258b\]]
[\[258b.1\]]gi mtshan nyid de/_des 'dod pa can rnams kyi tshul rab tu 'thob par 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen pos yang gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das 'di la gtan du mya ngan las mi 'da' ba gang lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po de la byang chub 
[\[258b.2\]]sems dpa' 'dod chen po ni chos thams cad thog ma nas mya ngan las 'das par shes nas/_gtan du mya ngan las mi 'da'i dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad spong ba'i 'dod chen po ni ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad spong ba'i 'dod chen po yang /_de bzhin gshegs 
[\[258b.3\]]pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyis brgya la gal te na dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su sbyor bar 'gyur ro/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_blo gros chen po 'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis ni sems can thams cad yongs su ma btang ngo /_/blo gros chen po de ltar de'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'i 'dod chen po rnams 
[\[258b.4\]]ni yongs su mya ngan las mi 'da'o/_/blo gros chen po'i tshig ni rnam par rtog pa dang tha dad pa yang ma yin gcig pa yang ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di lta ste/_de'i rgyu las 'byung ba'i mtshan nyid kyi phyir tshig gi rnam par rtog pa 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen po de ltar tshig 
[\[258b.5\]]'di rnam par rtog pa dang /_tha dad pa zhig yin na ni/_rnam par rtog pa'i rgyu las byung ba ma yin pas 'gyur ro/_/de ste gcig pa zhig yin na ni tshig de don gsal bar yang byed par yang mi 'gyur ba zhig na de ni byed do/_/de lta bas na tha dad pa yang ma yin/_gcig pa yang ma yin no/_/rnam 
[\[258b.6\]]rig 'di ni ming tsam ste/_/mtshan nyid kyis ni yod ma yin/_/blo gros chen po sangs rgyas kyi zhing la la ni mig mi 'dzums pas/_la la ni smin ma bskyod pa la sogs pas chos ston to/_/blo gros chen po nor ba de yang phyin ci log dang /_phyin ci log ma yin par rnam par brtags pas/_
[\[258b.7\]]'di lta ste 'phags pa'i rigs sam/_byis pa so so'i rigs dang rigs gnyis 'thob par byed pa yin no/_/blo gros chen po 'phags pa'i rigs kyang 'di lta ste/_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_sangs rgyas su dbye bas rnam pa gsum du dbye bar 'gyur ro/_/de la blo 
[\[259a\]]
[\[259a.1\]]gros chen po ji ltar na byis pa rnams kyis nor ba la rnam par brtags pas/_nyan thos kyi theg pa'i rigs bskyed ce na/_'di lta ste/_blo gros chen po rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid la mngon par zhen pas/_mngon par zhen pa nyan thos kyi theg pa'i rigs 
[\[259a.2\]]su 'gyur te/_de ltar na blo gros chen po nor ba de nyan thos kyi theg pa'i rigs 'thob par byed pa yin no/_/de la blo gros chen po ji ltar na nor pa de nyid la rnam par brtags pas/_rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa'i rigs 'thob par byed pa yin zhe na/_blo gros chen po 'di lta 
[\[259a.3\]]ste/_/nor ba de nyid kyis rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid la mngon par zhen pa dang /_'du 'dzi med pa'i phyir rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa'i rigs 'thob par byed pa yin no/_/blo gros chen po de la ji ltar na/_mkhas pa rnams kyis nor ba de nyid la rnam par brtags pas/_sangs rgyas kyi theg 
[\[259a.4\]]pa'i rigs 'thob par byed pa yin zhe na/_'di lta ste/_blo gros chen po rang gi sems snang ba tsam khong du chud pa'i phyir phyi rol la dngos po dang dngos po med par rnam par mi rtog pas rnam par brtags na sangs rgyas kyi theg pa'i rigs 'thob par byed pa yin no/_/de lta bas na blo gros chen 
[\[259a.5\]]po 'di ni rigs te rigs kyi don to/_/blo gros chen po byis pa rnams kyis nor ba la dngos po sna tshogs kyi rang bzhin du 'di ni de bzhin te/_gzhan du ma yin no zhes rnam par brtags na/_'khor ba'i theg pa'i rigs 'thob par byed do/_/blo gros chen po mya ngan las 'das pa ni 
[\[259a.6\]]rnam pa bzhi ste/_dngos po'i rang bzhin med pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa dang /_mtshan nyid sna tshogs kyi dngos po med pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa dang /_rang gi mtshan nyid kyi dngos po med par rtog pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa dang /_phung po rnams kyi rang dang spyi'i mtshan 
[\[259a.7\]]nyid kyi rgyun chad pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa'o/_/de dag ni blo gros chen po mu stegs can rnams kyi mya ngan las 'das pa yin te/_nga'i gsung rab ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po nga'i gsung rab la ni rnam par rtog pa dang bcas pa dang /_bcas pa'i yid kyi rnam 
[\[259b\]]
[\[259b.1\]]par shes pa bzlog pa ni mya ngan las 'das pa'o zhes bya'o/_/kun brtags pa yi rang bzhin yod/_/gzhan gyi dbang ni med pa ste/_/nor ba dag gis brtags pa snang /_/gzhan gyi dbang la mi rtog go/_/kun rdzob dang ni don dam ste/_/rgyu las skyes pa gsum du med/_/
[\[259b.2\]]brtags pa dag ni kun rdzob bshad/_/de bshad 'phags pa'i spyod yul lo/_/blo gros chen po ye shes ni rnam pa gsum ste/_'jig rten pa dang /_'jig rten las 'das pa dang /_'jig rten las 'das pa'i mchog go/_/de la skyes nas 'jig pa ni rnam par shes pa'o/_/
[\[259b.3\]]ma skyes mi 'jig pa ni ye shes so/_/gzhan yang blo gros chen po gsum 'dus pa'i bya ba skye ba dang ldan pa'i mtshan nyid ni rnam par shes pa'o/_/mi ldan pa'i ngo bo nyid ni ye shes so/_/blo gros chen po khyad par 'dod pa'i byang chub sems dpas de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po kun 
[\[259b.4\]]gzhi rnam par shes pa zhes bsgrags pa rnam par sbyar bar bya'o/_/de la ming dang mtshan ma ni yongs su kun brtags pa'i rang bzhin du shes par bya'o/_/gang yang blo gros chen po de la gnas shing bzhugs pa'i rnam par rtog pa sems dang sems las byung ba zhes pa bsgrags 
[\[259b.5\]]pa nyi ma dang 'od zer lhan cig pa bzhin du dus gcig tu byung ba sna tshogs kyi mtshan nyid rang gi rnam par rtog pa khong du chud par byed pa'i rang bzhin ni gzhan gyi dbang zhes bya'o/_/blo gros chen po yang dag pa'i ye shes dang /_de bzhin nyid ni mi 'jigs pa'i phyir yongs su grub par 
[\[259b.6\]]shes par bya'o/_/blo gros chen po gzhan yang gser dang rdo rje dang /_rgyal po'i rin sred dang /_thob pa dang khyad par dag ni mi 'jigs pa yin te/_blo gros chen po gal te mngon par rtogs pa thob pa yin na ni/_'phags pa rnams 'phags pa ma yin par 'gyur te/_'phags 
[\[259b.7\]]pa rnams ni 'phags pa ma yin par mi 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen po gser dang rdo rje ni rnam par gzhag kyang de bzhin du 'dug ste/_mi 'bri mi skye na byas pa ldem po ngag tu bshad pa la/_mi mkhas pa dag gis phyi nang gi chos thams cad skad cig pa'i don ji ltar brtags/_
[\[260a\]]
[\[260a.1\]]sems ni kun gzhi rnam shes te/_/ngar sems nyid ni yid yin no/_/gang gis yul rnams 'dzin pa ni/_/de ni rnam par shes zhes bya/_/sems ni rtag tu lung ma bstan/_/yid ni gnyi gar gyur pa ste/_/rnam par shes pa 'byung ba yang 
[\[260a.2\]]de ni dge dang mi dge ba'o/_/lnga po snang la rtog par byed/_/chos lnga dag dang rang bzhin rnams/_/de ni sangs rgyas spyod yul yin/_/yod pa'i zla grangs med pa ste/_/med pa'ang yod pa'i zla grangs so/_/kun du brtags dang gzhan gyi dbang /_/phan tshun mtshan 
[\[260a.3\]]nyid tha mi dad/_/gzugs kyi mi rtag nyid bzhin te/_/gcig la gcig kyang skyed byed pa'o/_/brtags pa shul du mthong gyur na/_/gzhan gyi dbang ni mi skye'o/_/gzhan gyi dbang ni rab mthong na/_/brtags pa de bzhin nyid du 'gyur/_/ji ltar 'phags pa rnam bsgom ltar/_/
[\[260a.4\]]dngos po'i dngos nyid med mod kyi/_/ji ltar byis pas brtags pa ltar/_/dngos po dag ni yod pa'o/_/sems ni snang dang rnam bral ba/_/rang bzhin gnyis kyang spangs pa ste/_/gnas rnams rab tu gyur pa ni/_/skye ba med par ngas bshad do/_/nam yang stong bas stong pa 
[\[260a.5\]]nyid/_/skye ba med pa'i stong pa'o/_/ji srid sems ni 'jug pa'i bar/_/de srid 'jig rten rgyang pan yin/_/'jig rten rang gi sems mthong na/_/rnam par rtog pa mi 'jig go/_/nga dang de bzhin gshegs rnams kyis/_/stong phrag gsum gyi mdo rnams kyis/_/mya ngan 'das 
[\[260a.6\]]pa shin tu bshad/_/'dod pa'i khams dang gzugs med du/_/sangs rgyas rnam par 'tshang mi rgya/_/gzugs kyi khams kyi 'og min du/_/'dod chags bral khyod 'tshang rgya'o/_/reg pa dang ni gzir ba yis/_/lha dang sems can dmyal ba'i lus/_/bar ma 
[\[260a.7\]]do yi srid med de/_/rnam par shes pas bskyed pa yin/_/mngal sgong drod gsher la sogs pa'i/_/srid pa bar ma 'byung ba dag_/sems can mang po sna tshogs bzhin/_/dam tshig gsum gyi rgyal po las/_/thang las skyes pa'i rin po che'i ri rnams dang /_
[\[260b\]]
[\[260b.1\]]chu las skyes pa'i rin po che'i ri rnams dang /_/thang las skyes pa'i rin po che la sogs pa phyogs bcu na rnam par gnas pa nga yir bya ba med par yongs su gzung ba med pa rnams la 'bul lo/_/don dam pa'i chos kyi rnam par rgyal ba las 'byung ba/_kha cig ni mngal na 
[\[260b.2\]]gnas pa nyid kyi tshe srin bu rnams kyis zos so/_/drang srong chen po yang 'di lta ste/_bskal pas bsregs pa 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi thabs mkhas pa ste/_drang srong chen po 'di lta ste dper na/_gser la gnod pa bya ba'i phyir mer bcug pa ma yin gyi/_
[\[260b.3\]]gser ma dul ba 'di ji ltar dul bar 'gyur snyam nas de'i phyir mer 'jug go/_/drang srong chen po de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyang rgyu med par bsreg pa'i bskal pa mi 'byin te/_'dir sems can gang la yang gnod par mi 'gyur ro/_/bskal pas bsregs 
[\[260b.4\]]pa'i 'jigs pa las bdag nyid bsregs pa mthong nas dga' ba dang /_mchog tu dga' ba skyes nas 'di skad ces bdag kyang 'di ltar gyur ces smon lam 'debs so/_/sems can gnas gang du 'du bar gyur pa bdag la bka' stsol cig_/bcom ldan 'das kyis 
[\[260b.5\]]bka' stsal pa/_drang srong chen po theg pa chen po gcig gis 'byung ba las sems can 'du bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub kyi snying po la sems can 'du bar 'gyur ro/_/phung po ma lus pa'i dbyings su sems can rnams 'du bar 'gyur ro/_/drang srong chen po yang 'di lta ste dper na/_chu klung tha dad pa 
[\[260b.6\]]dang /_klung phran dang chen po dag rgya mtsho chen por bab nas ro gcig tu gyur to/_/de bzhin du sems can gyi khams ji snyed pa'i/_dri ma med par grags pas bstan pa las/_thabs kyis ma zin pa'i shes rab ni bcings pa'o/_/thabs kyis zin pa'i shes rab ni grol ba'o/_/shes rab 
[\[260b.7\]]kyis ma zin pa'i thabs ni bcings pa'o/_/shes rab kyis zin pa'i thabs ni grol ba'o/_/de la thabs kyis ma zin pa shes rab bcings pa gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_stong pa nyid dang /_mtshan ma med pa dang /_smon pa med pa la nges par sems shing /_mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po 
[\[261a\]]
[\[261a.1\]]dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rgyan dang /_sems can yongs su smin par bya ba la mi sems pa ni thabs kyis ma zin pa'i shes rab ste bcings pa'o/_/de la thabs kyis zin pa'i shes rab dgrol ba gang zhe na/_mtshan la sogs pa la nges par sems shing /_stong pa 
[\[261a.2\]]mtshan ma med pa dang /_smon pa med pa la 'dris par bya ba 'di ni thabs kyis zin pa'i shes rab ste grol ba'o/_/de la shes rab kyis ma zin pa'i thabs bcings pa gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_lta ba thams cad dang /_nyon mongs pa kun nas ldang ba dang /_bag la nyal ba dang /_rjes su 
[\[261a.3\]]chags pa dang /_khong khro ba la gnas pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba rtsom pa thams cad byang chub tu mi bsngo ba ni/_shes rab kyis ma zin pa'i thabs te bcings pa'o/_/de la shes rab kyis zin pa'i thabs grol ba gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_lta ba thams cad nyon mongs pa la sogs pa 
[\[261a.4\]]spangs pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba rtsom pa thams cad byang chub tu yongs su bsngos la/_des kyang bsnyems pa med pa de ni shes rab kyis zin pa'i thabs te grol ba'o/_/kun dga' bo gang na byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa byed pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnams 
[\[261a.5\]]kyang yod do/_/gang na 'od dang gang na byang chub kyi shing dang /_gang na de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan dang /_gang na chos dang /_gang na zhal zas la sogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis mdzad pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing yod do/_/'phags pa rin po che'i mtha' 
[\[261a.6\]]las/_des ji skad bstan pa la gal te mtshungs pa med pa byed pa de ltar mos te/_mtshams med pa yang de lta bu'i dbyings yin par shes na/_de kun nas nyon mongs pa can du mi 'gyur ro/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'jam dpal mtshams med pa ni chos kyi dbyings su 
[\[261a.7\]]mtshungs par mnyam pa'i phyir te/_'di ltar mtshams med pa'i dbyings dang chos kyi dbyings ni gnyis su med cing gnyis su byar med do/_/lhag pa'i bsam pa bskul ba byas la/_byams pa byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa pa'i gang zag chos bzhi dang ldan na/_lnga brgya 
[\[261b\]]
[\[261b.1\]]tha ma la dam pa'i chos rnam par 'jig pa'i tshe/_ma snad cing ma smas la bde bar yongs su thar bar 'gyur ro/_/bzhi gang zhe na/_bdag gis 'khrul pa la so sor rtogs dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa pa'i gang zag gzhan gyi nyes pa mi gleng ba dang /_
[\[261b.2\]]mdza' bshes kyi khyim dang /_slong mo ster ba'i khyim la mi lta ba dang /_yid du mi 'ong ba'i tshig spong ba'o/_/yang bzhi ste/_thos pa nyung ngu'i sems can yongs su spong ba dang /_'khor mi bsdu ba dang bas mtha'i gnas mal la brten pa dang /_bdag dul ba dang /_
[\[261b.3\]]zhi ba dang /_zhi gnas kyi sbyor ba la brtson pa'o/_/byams pa nga ni tshul 'chos pa la dgon pa na gnas pa zhes mi smra'o/_/nga ni bsod nams chung ngu la 'dod pa chung ba zhes mi smra'o/_/nga ni kha zas bzang po 'dod pa la bsod snyoms pa zhes mi smra'o/_/nga ni gos sdu 
[\[261b.4\]]gu 'dod pa la phyag dar khrod pa zhes mi smra'o/_/nga ni khyim pa dang rab tu byung ba 'dres pa la ma 'dzings pa zhes mi smra'o/_/nga ni g.yo sgyu can la sangs rgyas 'byung ba dang phrad pa zhes mi smra'o/_/nga ni gzhan gyi 'khrul pa tshol ba la rnal 'byor la brtson pa zhes mi smra'o/_/

[\[271a.3\]]bsrung bar bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa mya ngan las 'das nas dus ngan pa'i tshe mu ge'i nyes pas phal cher rab tu byung zhing mgo reg tu gyur te/_de dag gis dge slong tshul khrims dang /_yon tan dang ldan pa dag skrod par 'gyur/_gsod par 'gyur/_dge 'dun rnam pa gsum 
[\[271a.4\]]ste/_tshul khrims 'chal pa dang /_byis pa dang /_rang bzhin gyi dge 'dun no/_/bud+d+ha dpyid zla tha [{]chungs [,]chung [}]nya'i nang bar du mya ngan las 'das te/_da phyin chad nga'i nyan thos rnams sha za bar mi gnang ngo /_/sha za na ni byams pa chen po chad par 'gyur ro zhes ngas bstan to/_/rigs 
[\[271a.5\]]kyi bu yang rung ba ma yin pa'i 'bras 'tshed pa dang /_sha dang nya sha len pa dang /_snabs su sbyor ba dang /_'bru mar gyi snod dang /_gdugs dang /_lha byed pa dang /_rgyal po la brten pa dang /_mo yig 'chad ba dang /_sman dpyad la brtson pa dang /_bran dang gser la sogs pa len 
[\[271a.6\]]pa dang /_ri mo dang /_gzugs byed pa dang /_skye shing 'dzugs pa dang /_gsang sngags dang /_sman dang /_byis pa'i rtsed mo dang /_gar thabs kyi yan lag dang /_'gros 'chos pa dang /_sgeg pa'i yan lag la brtson pa dag 'byung bar 'gyur te/_de dag spangs pa la dge slong zhes

[\[271b\]]
[\[271b.1\]]pa ni gsor mi rung ba'i nad 'dod chen po ma gtogs par nad thams cad gso bar mdzad do/_/gang mya ngan las 'da' ba de dag gso ba yin no/_/de bzhin gshegs pa ni sha'i sku yin te/_bud shing zad pa'i me bzhin du yongs su mya ngan las 'das so zhes bya ba 'di ni/_nyan

[\[271b.4\]]pa'i mdo chen po las/_gnyis su med pa la 'jug pa'i snang ba dang /_de nyid bdag yod pa dang /_bdag med pa gnyis su med par ngas bstan pa de rjes su dran par gyis shig_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po thog mnas bcos ma ma yin la/_nyon mongs pa ni glo bur ba yin te/_sangs rgyas su

[\[272b\]]

[\[280a.3\]]spyod sgrub pa dang /_'dod pa'i rigs su skye ba dang /_ser sna tshar gcod pa dang /_gtong ba'i rgyun 'phel bar 'gyur ba dang /[ {]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]kyi sgo gcod pa dang /_thun mong gi longs spyod las snying po len pa dang /_yo byad rdzogs par 'gyur ba dang /_bdog pa la goms par 'gyur 
[\[280a.4\]]ba dang /_'dzin pa'i sems med par 'gyur ba dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i bka' dang mthun pa dang /_sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa rdzogs par 'gyur ro/_/bcu gnyis mthong bas tshul khrims bsrung ste/_byin gyi rlabs phun sum tshogs par 'gyur ba dang /_byang chub

[\[281b.1\]]pa mi nyams pa dang /_sems can kun la byams pa dang /_sems can mi gtong ba dang /_byang chub la 'dun pa brtan pa dang /_brtson pa mi gtong ba dang /_dge ba'i bshes gnyen la brten cing bsnyen bkur byed pa dang /_chos la gus pa dang /_thos pa mang pos chog mi shes pa dang /_

[\[304a.1\]]slad du ma mchis pa ma lags pa la mchis zhes bgyi/_bka' stsal pa/_gzugs stong pa bco brgyad kyi phyir yod pa ma yin pa zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin du bsgre'o/_/de nas gnas brtan rab 'byor 'di snyam du gyur te/_'di ltar lha rnams kyis mngon 
[\[304a.2\]]par gtor ba'i me tog 'di rnams ni bdag gis sngon chad lha'i gnas thams cad na yang yod par ma mthong ste/_me tog 'di dag ni sprul pa'o/_/'di ltar lha'i bu rnams kyis mngon par gtor ba'i me tog 'di rnams ni ldum bu las skyes pa ma yin/_chu las skyes pa 
[\[304a.3\]]ma yin/_thang las skyes pa ma yin no/_/'di ltar me tog 'di dag ni yid las byung ba ste/_me tog 'di dag ni ldum bu las byung ba ma yin no snyam mo/_/kau shi kas gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das gnyis po 'di dag ni chos nyid de gang lags she na/_'dus byas kyi chos thams cad 
[\[304a.4\]]kyi chos nyid dang /_'dus ma byas kyi chos rnams kyi chos nyid do/_/de la 'dus byas kyi chos rnams kyi chos nyid gang lags she na/_nang stong pa nyid kyi ye shes nas 'jig rten pa'i chos rnams kyi ye shes dang /_'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos rnams kyi ye shes kyi bar lags so/_/
[\[304a.5\]]de la 'dus ma byas kyi chos rnams kyi chos nyid gang lags she na/_de ni gang la skye bam mchis pa dang /_'gag pa dang /_gnas pa dang /_gnas pa las gzhan du 'gyur ba dang /_kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /_rnam par byang ba dang /_'phel ba dang /_'grib pa ma mchis pa ste/_de 
[\[304a.6\]]ni chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid do/_/byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa la zhugs pa'i rigs kyi bu'am rigs kyi bu mo'i rmi lam na byang chub kyi snying po la 'dug gi bar du/_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bsgom pa dang /_bsam gtan la snyoms par 'jug pa dang /_brtson 'grus rtsom pa dang /_
[\[304a.7\]]bzod pa sgrub pa dang /_tshul khrims srung ba dang /_sbyin pa sbyin pa'i rigs kyi bu'am/_rigs kyi bu mo de yang bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la bsnyen par rig par bgyi na/_bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang dngos su shes rab 
[\[304b\]]
[\[304b.1\]]kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bsgom pa la sogs pa bsnyen pa lta smos kyang ci 'tshal/_stug po bkod pa las/_lus can rnams kyi lus kyi yul/_/stug na gnas la stug na med/_/zhing de dri med bsod nams te/_/stug po snying po shin tu mdzes/_/de bzhin thar pa thar shes 
[\[304b.2\]]la/_/zhing gi nang na zhing gi mchog_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi zhing rab ste/_/stug po de ni yul du brtag_/mnga' rnams bcu po de dag dang /_/stobs dang mngon mkhyen ting nge 'dzin/_/yid kyi rgyu las byung ba'i skus/_/sangs rgyas de ni rnam par mdzes/_/stug po thar 
[\[304b.3\]]mchog ye shes te/_/chos kyi dbyings dang rdzu 'phrul yang /_/stug ste rnal 'byor can gyi gnas/_/mun pa'i rgya las rab 'das nas/_/stug po'i zhing ni rnam par mdzes/_/phyogs 'di bka' las grub pa ste/_/rgyu can rnams kyi spyod yul min/_/rdul phran rnam par gzhig 
[\[304b.4\]]pa dang /_/rdul gyi dngos po mi gnas te/_/rang bzhin kun gyi mchog yin pas/_/rdul phran mchog ces de phyir brjod/_/dbang gi yon tan brgyad po rnams/_/phra sogs che ba'i yon tan dang /_/'thob dang 'dod 'byor bsgrags pa dang /_/bdag po dbang dang ldan pa dang /_/
[\[304b.5\]]ji ltar 'dod par rung ba rnams/_/thob nas stug po'i dbus yul na/_/rnam par gnas shing rab tu rtse/_/de ltar kun gzhi rnam shes kyang /_/rtag tu bag chags ldan gyur pa/_/rnam par shes pa rnam bdun po/_/'grogs kyang 'dre ba med pa ste/_/lha dang lha min 
[\[304b.6\]]'bar ba dang /_/lam rgyud kun kyang de yi dbang /_/sangs rgyas kun gyi zhing mdzes pa'ang /_/de nyid rgyur ni gyur ba ste/_/tshe dang drod dang rnam shes 'dis/_/gang tshe lus 'di bor gyur pa/_/de tshe lus 'di bor ba ste/_/shes bzhin tshor ba med pa'o/_/de ni kun gzhi rnam shes te/_/
[\[304b.7\]]yid ni dro bar rab tu brtags/_/yid kyi rnam shes de na ste/_/lhag ma dbang po lngar yang gnas/_/yid ches lung dang mi ldan par/_/chos rnams ston par byed pa ni/_/mtshan nyid rnam par rtog pa yis/_/bdag kyang chud gzon gzhan chud gzon/_/'jig rten rdul phran 
[\[305a\]]
[\[305a.1\]]bsags pa min/_/phyogs dang gnas su mi 'gro'o/_/de dag sor gnyis sor gsum du/_/ci yang rtsom par mi byed do/_/lha yul gnas nas gnas pa la/_/sna tshogs sar yang de bzhin rtogs/_/phung po gang zag bdag med par/_/rab tu rnam phye mthong gyur 
[\[305a.2\]]cing /_/chos kyi dngos po yong ma shig_/nyan thos de dag mngon rtogs 'gyur/_/ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po las/_/sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa/_'di ltar bcom ldan 'das de ni zhes bya ba la sogs pa 'byung /_ston pa bde bar gshegs pas bshad pa ltar/_/nges don mdo sde dag ni bye brag 
[\[305a.3\]]shes/_/gang las sems can gang zag skyes bu bstan/_/chos de thams cad drang ba'i don du shes/_/'jig rten dag na ting 'dzin sgom byed kyang /_/de ni bdag tu 'du shes 'jig mi byed/_/de yi nyon mongs phyir yang rab tu 'byung /_/lhag spyod kyis ni ting 'dzin 'dir bsgom bzhin/_/

[\[305a.5\]]legs bsnyen kyang /_/de dag phyir yang dgra dang 'dra bar 'gyur/_/byis pa dang ni byis pa mthun 'byor ba/_/ji ltar mi gtsang ba dang mi gtsang bzhin/_/mkhas pa dang ni mkhas pa'i skye bo dang /_/mthun zhing 'byor pa mar dang snying khu bzhin/_/byis pa la ni gang phan snying brtse ba/_/de 
[\[305a.6\]]la gnod pa byung ba byis pa dga'/_/de lta'i nyes phyir byis pa rnams spangs te/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]bzhin du gcig pu dgon par gnas/_/mkhas pa 'di 'dra'i nyes pa rig nas ni/_/shes rab dman bsten bdag gi mtho ris nyams/_/byang chub 'thob par ga la 'gyur snyam ste/_/
[\[305a.7\]]byis pa rnams dang nam yang yangs mi bsten/_/bla med mi 'jigs sbyin pa khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/gzhan phan sems can gnyen brtse khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/stobs bcu bden pa'i rtsal mnga' khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/bde gshegs mi mnyam mnyam pa khyod la phyag 'tshal 
[\[305b\]]
[\[305b.1\]]lo/_/mgon po shin tu thugs rje can la phyag 'tshal lo/_/dpa' bo dgra bzhi rnam par btul la phyag 'tshal lo/_/rtsod pa dam pa'i don rnams mkhyen la phyag 'tshal lo/_/chos kyi sku mchog mnga' pa'i lha la phyag 'tshal lo/_/snying rje byams 'od rgyal ba'i nyi ma mun sel ba/_/
[\[305b.2\]]shes rab shin tu yangs pa nam mkha'i dkyil du shar/_/don dam shin tu zab pa'i chos rnams ston mdzad pa/_/lha khyod la ni rtag tu bdag kyang skyabs su mchi/_/'gro ba 'di dag thams cad sangs rgyas 'gyur/_/'di na snod min sems can 'ga' yang med/_/bde gshegs yon tan 
[\[305b.3\]]de dag dpag med de/_/mi mchog yon tan rab kyi pha rol phyin/_/yon tan khyad par kun gyi pha rol son/_/sangs rgyas bsod nams zhing la mgos phyag 'tshal/_/zla 'od gzhon nur gyur pas/_bcom ldan 'das 'khor dang bcas pa bzhugs par rig nas/_mchod yon chen 
[\[305b.4\]]po rim gyis gsol te/_zhal zas gsol zhing bdar to/_/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' dag_/bag chags rab tu spangs gyur pa/_/de dag gzugs kyis mi 'phrogs te/_/sangs rgyas rigs la de dag gnas/_/bka' stsal pa/_zla 'od gzhon nur gyur pa 'di nyid kyis/_/
[\[305b.5\]]sngon yang rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer 'dir/_/sangs rgyas dag kyang bye ba phrag stong mthong /_/sku ni byang chub mtshan nyid de/_/sangs rgyas zhing yang de 'dra zhing /_/stobs dang rnam thar bsam gtan yang /_/de dag thams cad mtshan nyid gcig_/de ltar sangs rgyas 'byung 'gyur 
[\[305b.6\]]te/_/'jig rten mgon po de 'dra bas/_/sus kyang sha yi mig gis ni/_/nam yang mthong bar mi nus so/_/dpa' bo chen po chos kyi sku/_/sku ni chos las byung ba ste/_/gzugs kyi sku ni nam du yang /_/sangs rgyas gdags par nus ma yin/_/sangs rgyas kyi ni 
[\[305b.7\]]sku mthong nas/_/'dren pa mthong zhes smra mod kyi/_/nga ni gzugs kyi sku lus su/_/sus kyang blta bar mi nus so/_/gang gis 'khrul pa ci mthong yang /_/de dag gi ni nyes mi brjod/_/ci 'dra ba yi las byas pa/_/'bras bu de 'dra 'thob par 'gyur/_/dgon 
[\[306a\]]
[\[306a.1\]]pa la gnas pa lhur len pa'i phan yon ni bcu ste/_bya ba nyung bar gnas pa dang /_'du ba ring ba bas kyang ring du spong ba dang /_de la rtsod pa med par 'gyur ba dang /_gnod pa med par gnas pa dang /_zag pa 'phel bar mi byed pa dang /_rtsod pa'i gzhir 
[\[306a.2\]]mi byed pa dang /_shin tu zhi bar spyod pa dang /_shin tu bsdams par gnas pa dang /_sems thar pa dang mthun pa'i rgyun du 'gyur ba dang /_myur du rnam par grol ba mngon du byed pa'o/_/lha'i 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal srid kyi bde ba spangs te rab tu 'byung ngo zhes gzhon nu khyod kyis de 
[\[306a.3\]]ltar bslab par bya'o/_/rab tu byung nas kyang sbyangs pa'i yon tan yo byad bsnyungs pa la gnas shing /_bden par gnas pa dang /_bzod pa dang des pa phun sum tshogs par bya'o/_/sngon sangs rgyas gzi brjid tshogs rgyal gyis chos bshad nas/_rgyal po brtan 
[\[306a.4\]]byin rab tu byung ngo /_/de nas 'dzam bu'i gling gi mi rim gyis rab tu byung ba dang /_sangs rgyas kyi mthus 'bras ma rmos ma btab par 'khrung ba dang /_chos gos kyang rang byung /_g.yog kyang lha rnams kyis byas so/_/'jig rten khams ni stong dag tu/_/ngas ni mdo sde gang bshad 
[\[306a.5\]]pa/_/tshig 'bru tha dad don gcig ste/_/thams cad brjod par nus ma yin/_/tshig gcig rnam par bsgoms na ni/_/de dag thams cad bsgom par 'gyur/_/sangs rgyas ji snyed thams cad kyis/_/chos rnams mang po rab bshad pa/_/chos rnams thams cad stong pa nyid/_/
[\[306a.6\]]chos kyi rang bzhin shes pas na/_/de phyir sangs rgyas zhes bya ste/_/sems can bsam gyis mi khyab cing /_/dpag tu med pa byang chub 'dul/_/chos rnams mig yor lta bu yang /_/gang gis ngo bo nyid shes pa/_/de dag gzugs kyi lus su ni/_/sangs rgyas sku la mi lta'o/_/chos

[\[306b.1\]]mthong /_/'khor ba dag ni 'phel byed cing /_/so so'i skye bo de dag 'khor/_/so so tha dad 'gro bas na/_/so so'i skye bor brjod pa yin/_/'du shes 'di ni spang snyam du/_/gang gi 'du shes 'byung ba ni/_/'du shes spros la spyod pa ste/_/'du shes las ni de mi thar/_/su yis 'du shes

[\[306b.5\]]rten gyi yang mchod rten 'byung /_/de bzhin du pad+ma la sogs pa sprul nas/_de'i dri tshor ba dang de las sgra grag pa mnyan pas kyang dug gsum spong ba la sogs pa'i yon tan 'byung ngo /_/byang chub sems dpa' bde byin gyis de bzhin gshegs pa dbyangs byin la mchod par lag pa g.yas pa

[\[306b.7\]]'byung /_chos dang zang zing sbyin pa ni bsngo ba bzhin byas te/_thabs mkhas pa gang gis de bzhin gshegs pa rnams mngon par sangs rgyas pa'i thabs de thob pa dang /_bla na med pa'i byang chub myur du thob pa ston par 'gyur ba'i dge ba'i bshes gnyen dang phrad pa dang /_longs spyod gang gis sems can 
[\[307a\]]
[\[307a.1\]]thams cad 'tsho bar 'gyur ba'i longs spyod de dag dang phrad pa dang /_bdag gi lus gang gis sems can thams cad la chos dang zang zing gis phan 'dogs pa 'thob par 'gyur ba'i lus 'dzin par bsngo ba'o/_/gcig la gcig kyang gnod sems byas

[\[313a.7\]]ma dang /_shin tu 'grel pa rnams mthong nas/_de'i rang bzhin la mngon par zhen pa'i rnam par rtog pa 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen po 'di ni byang chub sems dpas khong du chud nas bzlog par bya'o/_/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la blo gros chen pos gsol ba/_ji ltar 
[\[313b\]]
[\[313b.1\]]bdag dang byang chub sems dpa' gzhan sgro 'dogs pa dang /_skur pa'i lta ba ngan pa spangs pa'i blo can rnams bla na med pa'i byang chub tu 'tshang rgya zhing /_sangs rgyas nas kyang rtag pa dang sgro 'dogs pa dang chad pa dang /_skur pa 'debs pa'i lta ba rnam par spangs te/_khyod kyis sangs rgyas 
[\[313b.2\]]kyi tshul la mi skur par 'gyur ba sgro 'dogs pa dang /_skur pa'i mtshan nyid bdag la bshad du gsol/_bka' stsal pa/_sgro 'dogs pa dang skur pa dag_/sems tsam po la yod pa min/_/lus dang longs spyod gnas lta bus/_/sems ni su dag mi shes pa/_/sgro 'dogs pa dang skur ba 
[\[313b.3\]]la/_/mi mkhas de dag rab tu spyod/_/rang bzhin med pa ni rang bzhin thar pa med pa la dgongs te/_dngos po thams cad rang bzhin med pa zhes bya'o/_/blo gros chen po thams cad kyi rten nas rgyu dang 'brel par 'byung ba'i mtshan nyid ni rnam pa gnyis te/_'di ltar phyi'i dang nang gi 
[\[313b.4\]]gi'o/_/de ltar phyi'i rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba ni 'jim pa'i gong bu dang /_lag zungs dang /_'khor lo dang /_srad bu dang /_chu dang /_mi'i nan tan la sogs pa'i rkyen gyis rdza ma 'byung ba dang /_de bzhin du thags las snam bu dang /_rtsi skyong las re lde dang /_sa bon las 
[\[313b.5\]]myu gu dang /_srub ma la sogs pa mi'i nan tan dang /_zho las mar 'byung ba 'di ni phyi'i rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba snga ma las phyi ma 'byung ba'o/_/nang gi ni 'di lta ste/_ma rig pa dang /_srid pa dang /_las dang /_chos 'di dag la sogs pa ni rten cing 'brel 
[\[313b.6\]]par 'byung ba'i ming 'thob po/_/blo gros chen po 'di dag las skyes pa'i phung po dang /_khams dang skye mched ces bya ba'i chos de dag kyang rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i ming 'thob ste/_de dag ni khyad par med pa las byis pa rnams kyis brtags pa'o/_/blo 
[\[313b.7\]]gros chen pos gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das kyis rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba bshad pas/_rgyu rnam par brjod pa nyid bgyis kyi/_rang gi tshul gyis slar gzhil ba'i tshul ma gsungs na/_bcom ldan 'das mu stegs byed rnams kyang gang las dngos po rnams 'byung 
[\[314a\]]
[\[314a.1\]]ba 'di lta ste/_gtso bo dang dbang po dang /_skyes bu dang dus dang /_rdul phra mo 'jug pas/_rgyu las byung bar brjod de/_bcom ldan 'das kyis ni rnam grangs gzhan gyis dngos po rnams skye bar bka' stsal mod kyi/_grub pa'i mtha' la bye brag tu dbye ba ni 
[\[314a.2\]]ma mchis so/_/bcom ldan 'das mu stegs can rnams kyang ma mchis pa dang /_ma mchis pa las skye bar brjod/_bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang ma mchis pa las rkyen rnams kyis 'byung ba dang /_byung nas kyang 'jig par gsungs te/_gang yang bcom ldan 'das kyis ma rig 
[\[314a.3\]]pa'i rkyen gyis rga shi'i bar gsungs pa 'di yang rgyu med par smra ba rnams brjod pa gsungs kyi rgyur smra ba ni ma lags so/_/'di yod na 'di yod do zhes bgyi ba 'di ni cig car gnas pa rnams la rung gi/_rim gyis 'byung ba la ltos bar gnas pa rnams la ma yin te/_mu stegs kyi brjod 
[\[314a.4\]]pa nyid bzang gi khyod kyi bzang po ma lags so/_/ci'i slad du zhe na/_mu stegs kyi rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba ma lags pa'i rgyus 'bras bu mngon par sgrub po/_/bcom ldan 'das khyod kyis rgyu yang 'bras bu la ltos/_'bras bu yang rgyu la ltos pas/_rgyu rkyen yang 'dre ste/_de bzhin phan tshun thug 
[\[314a.5\]]pa ma mchis par yang 'gyur ro/_/'di yod na 'di yod do zhes brjod pa ni 'jig rten rgyu ma mchis par 'gyur ro/_/bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po gzung ba dang /_'dzin pa med pa dang /_rang gi sems snang ba tsam du khong du chud pa'i phyir 'di yod do zhes brjod kyang rgyu dang byed par smra ba dang /_rgyu 
[\[314a.6\]]rkyen 'dres par yang mi 'gyur ro/_/gang gzung ba dang /_'dzin pa la mngon par zhen cing /_phyi rol gyi rang gi yul gyi dngos po med par rang gi sems snang ba tsam du khong du chud pa de dag la ni 'di lta bu nyes par 'gyur gyi/_nga'i rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba brjod pa la nyes

[\[314b\]]
[\[314b.1\]]ba tsam du ma rtogs pa rgyu dang rkyen gyi byed pa las skye ba'i mtshan nyid du mngon par chags pa rnams la phan pa'i phyir chos thams cad sgyu ma dang /_rmi lam gyi rang bzhin gyi mtshan nyid du bstan to/_/blo gros chen po chos thams cad ni ngo bo nyid med pa ste/_

[\[314b.3\]]pa ni mu stegs can sems tsam mi shes pa yin gyi/_sangs rgyas kyi ma yin gyi bud+d+ha'i ni gzhag par lung bstan pa ye shes med de snod la ltos nas re zhig lung mi bstan par bya bar 'gyur/_log pa la bden par chags pas ni phung po lnga 'dus pa dang /_'byung

[\[314b.5\]]kyi/_'byung ba chen po rnams la ni med do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_blo gros chen po dngos po dang /_rtags dang /_mtshan nyid dang /_dbyings dang /_bya ba'i bdag nyid dang /_sbyor ba med pas 'byung ba rnams mi skye'o/_/dngos po la sogs pa'i bya ba 'dus pa las skye bar 
[\[314b.6\]]'gyur gyi/_rtags dang mi ldan pa las ma yin no/_/de lta bas na blo gros chen po 'byung ba dang /_'byung ba las gyur pa'i mtshan nyid ni/_mu stegs byed rnams kyis rnam par brtags pa yin te/_ngas ni ma yin no/_/brtags pa sna tshogs snang ba

[\[315a.1\]]blo gros chen po nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas thams cad ni shes bya'i sgrib ba dang /_las kyi bag chags ma spangs pa'i phyir chos la bdag med pa ma rtogs shing bsam gyis mi khyab par 'gyur ba'i 'chi 'pho ba ma thob pas nyan thos rnams la theg pa

[\[315b.1\]]sna tshogs sems las byung ba ste/_/mi rnams la ni phyi rol snang /_/'jig rten pa yi sems tsam mo/_/phyi rol snang ba yongs med de/_/sems ni sna tshogs rnams su snang /_/lus dang longs spyod gnas 'dra ba/_/sems tsam du nangs

[\[356a.2\]]gzir ba'i sems can gyi khams mthong nas snying rje chen po skyes te/_byang chub sems dpa' byang chub tu sems bskyed pa dang /_bsti stang du byas te sbyin pa bzang po yid du 'ong ba byin nas/_byang chub sems dpa' bdag nyid byang chub tu sems bskyed pa dang /_gzhan 
[\[356a.3\]]byang chub tu sems bskyed pa mthong nas/_byang chub sems dpa' byang chub tu sems bskyed pa dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i mtshan nyid dang dpe byad bzang po'i rgyan sna tshogs kyi yon tan bsngags pa rdzogs pa thos nas/_byang chub sems dpa' byang chub tu sems 
[\[356a.4\]]bskyed pa gong ma gsum ni/_sangs rgyas kyi byang chub srung ba yin te/_myur du bla na med pa'i byang chub las phyir mi ldog par 'gyur ro/_/lhag ma bzhi ni byang chub sems dpa' dmu rgod dam pa'i chos 'dzin pa ma yin par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[356a.5\]]chos lnga dang ldan na phyir mi ldog pa'i chos su rig par bya ste/_lnga ni sems can thams cad la sems snyoms pa dang /_pha rol gyi rnyed pa dang bkur sti la phrag dog med pa dang /_srog gi phyir yang dam pa'i chos 'dzin pa'i dge slong gi bsngags pa ma yin 
[\[356a.6\]]pa brjod pa dang /_shes kyi khe dang rnyed pa dang /_bkur sti dang grags pa la mi chags pa dang /_chos zab mo la mos pa'o/_/chos lnga dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' dmu rgod yin te/_lnga ni gzi brjid chung zhing bka' blo mi bde ba yin/_dman pa

[\[356b.1\]]ma'i tshe phyi ma'i dus na byang chub sems dpa' sgyid lug par mi 'gyur zhing /_yang dag par blangs pa ston la bsam ba thag pa nas byang chub la zhugs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' de dag ni sangs rgyas grangs med pa mang po la bsnyen bkur byas pa yin no/_/de dag gis ngan

[\[356b.4\]]min te/_/ji ltar de yis skye med brtags phyir ro/_/rnam rtog thams cad bdud kyi zhugs pa ste/_/'gyur med chos kyi phyag rgya zhes bya'o/_/skye ba med pa bden pa gcig yin na/_/de la kha cig bden pa bzhi zhes zer/_/byang chub snying por 'dug nas bden cig kyang /_/grub 
[\[356b.5\]]par ma mthong zhi lta ga la zhig_/sgyu ma lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin las/_de bzhin gshegs pa 'od dpag med mya ngan las 'das nas/_bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag brgyad cu rtsa bzhi'i bar du dam pa'i chos gnas par 'gyur ro/_/dam pa'i chos nub ma thag pa'i mtshan

[\[357a.1\]]rnam par nges pa'i cho 'phrul gyi ting nge 'dzin las/_byang chub sems dpa' gang gis snying rje dang ldan pa'i sems bskyed na dge ba'i rtsa ba des gal te sangs rgyas ma byung bar 'dzam bu'i gling du skyes na yang skyes bu dam par 'gyur/_/lha rnams kyis yongs su gzung

[\[357b.1\]]gis phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas bcu'i mtshan bzung na bla na med pa'i byang chub las phyir mi ldog bar 'gyur ba de lta bu bcom ldan 'das kyis bshad du gsol zhes sogs kyi lan du bka' stsal pa/_shar na 'jig rten gyi khams lag mthil ltar mnyam pa na sangs rgyas mya ngan med pa bzhugs/_

[\[360b.1\]]pa drug sgrub pa 'di ni byang chub kyi phyir yin na/_de la mi blun po dag 'di skad ces shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa kho nas byang chub sems dpa' bslab par bya'o zhes zer zhing pha rol tu phyin pa lhag ma rnams sun 'byin to/_/'di ji snyam du sems/_ka shi'i rgyal po gang yin pa des mgo

[\[361a.1\]]sems can dmyal ba na rtse bar byed de 'di ni byang chub sems dpa'i yul yin no/_/lha sbyin ni sems can dmyal bar 'gro ba yang ma yin no/_/lha mo slob dpon lung ston pa bram ze kauN+Da n+yi 'das pa'i 'og tu lo brgya nyi shu lon pa na mau rgya'i rigs zhes bya ba byed kyi

[\[374b.1\]]dpung las rgyal bar 'gyur/_/zhes 'byung /_'phags pa shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa khri brgyad stong ba las bka' stsal pa/_rab 'byor gal te sems can rnams dngos po yin te/_dngos po med pa ma yin na ni/_gang dag gi ched de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par 
[\[374b.2\]]rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba'i sems can de dag phung po'i lhag ma med pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings su yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar mi 'gyur ba zhig na/_rab 'byor gang gi phyir sems can rnams dngos po med cing dngos po yod pa ma 
[\[374b.3\]]yin pas de'i phyir gang dag gi ched du de bzhin gshegs pas chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba'i sems can de dag phung po lhag ma med pa'i mya ngan las 'da' ba'i dbyings su yongs su mya ngan las 'da'o/_/yongs su mya ngan las 'das so/_/yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar 
[\[374b.4\]]'gyur ro zhes 'byung ba dang /_yang rab 'byor gal te de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis dngos po yin te/_dngos po med pa ma yin na ni/_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i 
[\[374b.5\]]sangs rgyas kyi 'od dang /_sku mdog dang gzi brjid dang /_dpal gyis lha dang /_mi dang /_lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten zil gyis mnan te/_mdzes shing lham me/_lhan ne/_lhang nger mi 'gyur ba zhig na/_rab 'byor gang gi phyir de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag

[\[375a\]]
[\[375a.1\]]ni/_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis/_dge slong ngam/_bram ze'am/_lha'am/_bdud dam/_tshangs pa'am/_sus kyang 'jig rten du chos dang mthun par bskor bar mi nus pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo

[\[375a.3\]]rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas dge slong ngam/_bram ze'am/_lha'am/_bdud dam/_tshangs pa'am/_/sus kyang 'jig rten du chos dang mthun par bskor bar mi nus pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ro zhes 'byung ba dang /_yang rab 'byor gal te byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[375a.4\]]sems dpa' chen po'i rdo rje lta bu'i ye shes dngos po yin te/_dngos po med pa ma yin na ni/_theg pa chen po 'di byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos bag chags dang /_mtshams sbyor ba dang /_nyon mongs pa thams cad dngos po med par rtogs nas/_rnam pa 
[\[375a.5\]]thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi ye shes rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa thob nas/_lha dang /_mi dang /_lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten zil gyis mnan te/_nges par 'byung bar mi 'gyur ba zhig na/_rab 'byor gang gi phyir byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa'

[\[375b\]]
[\[375b.1\]]bshad pa na/_gal te byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po nem nur med cing /_rmongs pa med par gyur na/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de ni 'di lta bu ste/_sems can thams cad bskyab pa dang /_sems can thams cad yongs

[\[375b.7\]]yid la byed pa mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya ba med par rig par bya ba'i phyir ro zhes 'byung /_'phags pa sangs rgyas phal po che sdong po brgyan pa las/_byang chub sems dpa' spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi yon tan 'byung ba/_song shig nor [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dpal gyi chu rgyal

[\[376a.1\]]dpung shig na/_/mkhas pa rnams kyi ri rgyal gru 'dzin mdzes pa yod/_/rin chen rgyus byas shing dang nags tshal me tog mang /_/bza' shing ra ba'i rdzing dang chu bran 'bab par ldan/_/dri mchog de la brten nas spyan ras 
[\[376a.2\]]gzigs kyi dbang /_/'gro ba rnams la phan pa'i don du rnam par spyod/_/de gan song dang nor [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]khyod la mgon po yi/_/yon tan rgya chen tshul 'jug bstod par byed/_/g.yas phyogs bskor ba byas te gus par skur ston nas/_/nor [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]shin tu dul ba lho 
[\[376a.3\]]phyogs rgyud du song /_/spyan ras gzigs dbang drang srong snying rje rnam spyod pa/_/rin chen ri yi sul na gnas par des kyang mthong /_/rin chen sna tshogs rdo rje'i ri yi sman ljongs na/_/pad+ma'i snying po seng ge'i khri la brten pa 'dug_/lha dang lha min klu dang

[\[376b.1\]]thar bar 'gyur/_/rgyal po dag la nyes pa'i g.yor byas bsad spangs pa/_/de tshe de dag nga yi ming yang dran gyur na/_/mda' 'phangs de yi lus la phog par mi 'gyur ro/_/mtshon rnos btab kyang de dag mi 'jug rgal bar 'gyur/_/dbang po'i 'khor du

[\[376b.3\]]dgras 'jigs nam phyin te/_/seng ge dom gzig gcan gzan g.yag rgod sbrul gang yang /_/dgra rnams kun las rgyal zhing 'jigs pa med par 'gyur/_/gang rnams la las nga yi ming zhig dran byas na/_/sdang ba'i sems rgyun sa chen ltongs su

[\[376b.6\]]yi ming tsam dran par byas na thar pa thob/_/gang rnams shar gnyer sna tshogs glags lta ji snyed dang /_/gang rnams rtag tu zhe sdang mi snyan brjod pa rnams/_/nga yi ming thos dran byas snyan par brjod par 'gyur/_/mthong ba tsam gyi mod la byams
